# Recomputed paper numbers

AUC40 averages the first 40 chronological valid best-so-far errors. Invalid evaluations consume budget and cannot improve the incumbent. Sample SD uses n minus 1. Paired BCa intervals use 10,000 resamples with seed 20260812. Exact signed-rank tests discard exact zeros, rank ties by their average and enumerate the conditional sign distribution. Holm adjustment is applied to each study's two primary comparisons.

Per-run values and sensitivity analyses support the reported aggregates; a blank printed value means the scalar is not separately typeset.

## Values outside these records

- **Calibrated-motion figure caption (fig:calibrated_motion)**, realized 1.121 Hz: Requires the recording timebase and frequency fit, which are not in the compact calibration records.
- **Section V-E, open-loop hardware deployment**, 10 trials; all 10 positive signed displacements: These trials use separate hardware trajectories and policies from an earlier simulator.
- **Section V-E, open-loop hardware deployment**, +0.382 +/- 0.220 m signed initial-heading displacement: Requires the separate hardware trajectory measurements.
- **Section V-E, open-loop hardware deployment**, 0.566 +/- 0.344 m net displacement: Requires the separate hardware trajectory measurements.

## Record coverage

- Interpolation training histories are unavailable here. The archive contains the 32 terminal assessments, from which all printed interpolation outcomes are recomputed; no interpolation training run is included.
- The original fifteen-seed summary contains fourteen completed direct-update runs. The paper uses the later completed fifteen-run set, verified against its separate direct-result manifest and descriptives.
- Fixed settings such as the 40-call budget, sixteen parameters, eight nominal frequencies, frame count and step schedules are study definitions rather than estimated outcomes.
- The discussion's approximate 60 mm error and 10 percent of the 600 mm body length summarize the reported final-error means; body length is a measured robot dimension rather than a run outcome.
